        "div" : "<div xml:lang=\"de-DE\" xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml\" lang=\"de-DE\"><a name=\"QuestionnaireResponse_pcor-mii-exa-promis-16-response\"> </a><p>Beispielhafte PROMIS-16 Antwort eines hypothetischen Patienten über alle 8 Domänen (Physical Function, Anxiety, Depression, Fatigue, Sleep, Social Roles, Pain Interference, Cognition).
